🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus+ Staj Hareketliliği için minimum staj süresi **2 aydır**. Bu süre,
   hem lisans hem de lisansüstü öğrenciler için geçerlidir.   Başvuru
   yapabilmek için ev sahibi kuruluştan **minimum 2 aylık** staj süresini
   kapsayan bir "Kabul Belgesi" alınması gerekmektedir. Ayrıca, staj
   hareketliliği boyunca bu sürenin altına inilmemesi gerekiyor.   Özetle,
   Erasmus staj hareketliliğinde asgari süre kesinlikle **2 ay** olarak
   belirlenmiştir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (47.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program at Sabancı University:  1.
   **Requirements**:   - Obtain an acceptance letter from a host organization
   for a minimum 2-month internship.   - Meet the GPA requirement: **2.20 for
   undergraduates / 2.50 for graduate students**.   2. **Application Steps**:
   - Submit your documents (CV, transcript, and acceptance letter) to the
   Internship Office.   - Apply online through the **Erasmus+ Internship
   Mobility Application System** when applications open (announced on the
   internal web and internship website, typically by October).   3. **Post-
   Selection**:   - Approved students must sign the **Erasmus+ Internship
   Mobility Agreement** and complete the Learning Agreement, Grant Agreement,
   and insurance/travel documents.   4. **Grant**: Eligible students receive
   grants allocated based on their Erasmus+ score ranking.   **Note**: Check
   the university’s internal portals for deadlines and updates.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe hesaplaması şu şekilde yapılır:  1.
   Öğrencinin Erasmus+ puanı, TOEFL/YDS/ELAE gibi dil sınav sonuçlarının
   %50'si ile GNO'nun %50'si alınarak hesaplanır.  2. Hesaplanan Erasmus+
   puan sırasına göre öğrenciler listelenir ve kontenjan dahilinde sırayla
   hibe dağıtılır.  3. Hibe miktarı, Ulusal Ajans tarafından her yıl
   belirlenir ve ülke bazında aylık olarak ödenir.  4. Ödeme, Öğrenci Seçim

🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for exchange students involves several
   steps. The student prepares the LA, specifying the courses to be taken
   abroad, and submits it within the period announced by the International
   Relations Office (IRO). The Exchange Academic Advisor approves or requests
   changes to the course selections, SU equivalent credits, course types, and
   ECTS credit values. The student must ensure the LA is signed by both the
   Exchange Academic Advisor and the partner institution. Any changes to the
   course schedule must be approved by the Exchange Academic Advisor before
   completing the term abroad. After completing the exchange, the student
   must submit the partner university's transcript, the completed and signed
   LA, and a signed Certificate of Attendance to the IRO.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (41.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Students can object to disciplinary sanctions within **15 days** by
   submitting a written petition to the University Administrative Board. The
   board reviews and concludes the objection within **15 days**, either
   accepting or rejecting the sanction. If rejected, the disciplinary
   committee or supervisor re-examines the objection. For dormitory-related
   penalties, objections must be filed within **5 working days** (for
   removal) or **7 working days** (for warnings/reprimands). Students can
   also appeal to administrative courts without prior objection. During
   appeals, execution of the punishment is suspended if a stay order is
   issued.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (69.9s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The graduate program opening process requires an application to the
   Council of Higher Education (YOK) with the recommendation of the Graduate
   School Board, the opinion of the University Executive Committee (UEC)
   under the coordination of the Vice President for Education (VP-E), the
   Academic Council (AC) decision, and the approval of the Board of Trustees.
   For closure, the Graduate School Board submits a proposal with
   justifications, which is reviewed by the AC and then presented to the
   Board of Trustees for final approval. Changes to existing graduate
   programs, such as course requirements or credits, follow specific
   procedures for course modifications. Information about closed programs is
   stored to preserve student history, and updates are reflected in the
   YOKSIS system within one week of YOK approval.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (46.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   At Sabancı University, GPA (Grade Point Average) is calculated by
   multiplying the SU credit of each course by its coefficient, summing these
   values, and then dividing by the total SU credits of all courses taken.
   For undergraduate students, the GPA includes all courses taken, and the
   last grade for a repeated course is considered. For graduate students, the
   GPA is calculated based on courses taken in the related term, and the last
   grade for repeated courses is also taken into account. All GPA
   calculations are shown with two decimals, but for academic ranking, all
   digits after the decimal are considered.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (68.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   For undergraduate students, leave requests must be submitted by the 7th
   week (paying 50% of fees) or by the 4th week (paying 25% of fees)
   following the first day of classes in the academic year. Valid reasons
   include health, financial, family, personal, academic, or unexpected
   situations, requiring documentation.   Graduate students follow a similar
   process but must apply to the Graduate School Directorate with a petition
   if returning early, with any paid leave fees deducted from the current
   semester's tuition.   Special cases like conscription or arrest require
   application within 2 months, with leave approved from the start of the
   situation. For unpaid leaves, returning within 3 business days is
   mandatory to avoid contract termination.
------------------------------------------------------------

   programın staj yükümlülüğünü tamamlayabilir.  5. Türkiye'de staj
   yapacaklar, başlangıçtan en geç bir hafta önce SGK belgelerini OMS'ye
   yüklemelidir.   SSBF öğrencileri Akademik Staj için 3.00 GNO şartını

   As an undergraduate, graduate, or exchange student at Sabancı University,
   you can borrow **60 books** for **60 days**. The borrowing period can be
   renewed unlimited times unless another user requests the same book.   For

   days**.   Note: The renewal policy applies unless the book is requested by
   another user.

------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include:  1.
   **Warning** and **reprimand** (issued by Faculty Deans or Graduate School
   Directors).  2. **Temporary suspension** from the university (ranging from
   **1 week to 1 month**, or **1-2 semesters**).  3. **Dismissal** from the
   university.  4. **Removal from dormitories** (decided by the Disciplinary
   Board for severe violations).  Repeat offenses result in **one grade
   heavier penalties**, but dismissal cannot be imposed for repetition alone.
   Suspended students must **pay tuition** for the affected semester and are
   **banned from campus** during suspension.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (50.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The graduation audit process begins with prospective graduate students
   applying through the SIS within the dates specified in the academic
   calendar. The SR and Faculties/Institutes must reach full agreement and
   complete the implementation steps outlined in the Graduation Audit and
   Diploma Issuance Procedure. Students who do not qualify for graduation at
   the end of the semester must renew their applications for the next
   semester. The SR monitors and controls conditions such as courses,
   credits, and GPA every semester, while thesis-related requirements in
   graduate programs are monitored by the student, thesis advisor, and
   Institute. Graduation lists, approved by the Director of Graduate School,
   are submitted to the SR to determine eligible candidates. Diplomas are
   issued to students who complete all necessary procedures and are delivered
   personally, via notarized power of attorney, or by registered mail upon
   request.
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
   Here’s how the lost and found procedure works at Sabancı University:   1.
   Found items should be reported to the Security Office (Building D2) or by
   calling extension **3565**.  2. Security officers prepare a **two-copy
   Found Item Return Form**, signed by both the finder and the officer.  3.
   Items are categorized as **valuable** (e.g., electronics, wallets,
   precious metals) or **non-valuable**, tagged, and stored separately.  4.
   Unclaimed items are held for **1 year** before disposal.  5. In case of
   theft/loss on campus, notify the Security Unit (**Tel: 5555**) and IT Unit
   (via [suTicket](https://suticket.sabanciuniv.edu)); off-campus incidents
   require a police report.   Lost items are logged by the Security
   Directorate, which manages returns or disposal.

------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University provides several types of shuttle services:  1.
   **University Shuttle Services** for all university members, operating on
   routes and hours determined by the Transportation Services Unit (TS).  2.
   **Private Student Shuttles** to ensure coordination between students and
   the shuttle company.  3. **On-Campus Ring Service** for transportation
   within the campus, following routes and schedules set by the University
   Housing (UH).  4. **Special Service Requests** for specific purposes like
   artistic, sportive, academic, or social activities, arranged by university
   units and faculties.  5. **Laundry Shuttle Service** for collecting and
   delivering dirty linens to the campus, scheduled by Cleaning Services.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (109.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   No, a suspended student cannot transfer credits earned at another
   university back to Sabancı University. According to the Disciplinary
   Procedure, even if students who receive suspension or dismissal
   punishments take courses at another institution during their suspension
   period, these courses cannot be substituted into their Sabancı University
   program. The disciplinary measures explicitly prohibit credit transfer
   during suspension periods.   For general exchange programs (non-
   disciplinary cases), credit transfer requires approval through the
   Learning Agreement process and adherence to SU's grade conversion
   procedures, but this does not apply to disciplinary suspensions.

   disturb others from studying/resting, and must comply with all

   disciplinary action.  2. **Room Assignments**: Dormitory management

   student room allocations. Students cannot swap beds/wardrobes without
   management approval.  3. **Identification**: Residents must not share ID
   cards/rooms, must identify themselves to staff when requested, and refrain
   from misusing others' IDs.  4. **Duration of Stay**: Dormitories open 2
   days before each term starts and must be vacated within 2 days after the
   term ends. Late vacating twice without valid excuse bans dorm access next
   term.  5. **Visitors & Access**: Rules restrict visitor permissions and
   require residents to allow staff access to rooms when necessary.
   Unauthorized room transfers are prohibited.  6. **Suspensions**: Students
   suspended from the university cannot stay in dorms during their suspension
   period. Extended absences (>1 month) require formal exit procedures.

   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University are arranged by
   submitting the "Special Event and Catering Request Form" on SUForm at
   least 30 days before the event. The Food and Beverage Services (FBS) unit
   then provides a preliminary offer, including specifications and menus,
   based on the request. The requesting unit must approve these details and
   initiate the procurement process. FBS coordinates with pre-selected
   companies for execution, evaluates their suitability every 3 years, and
   oversees the event logistics. Events include meals, coffee breaks, and
   cocktails, but advance procurement for future dates is not permitted.

   arşiv araştırması formunu doldurup ilk hafta içinde teslim etmeleri
   gereklidir.   Not: Akciğer filmleri Verem Savaş Dispanseri (VSD) dışında

🤖 RAG ANSWER (56.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To qualify for the Rectorate Research Fund at Sabancı University,
   applicants must:  1. Be full-time faculty members who started at Sabancı
   University within the last two years and have completed at least six
   months at the institution.  2. Either submit at least two new documented
   external grant project applications **or** secure at least one guaranteed
   external financing by the project's end.  3. Have published high-impact
   scientific articles.  4. The project must align with their ongoing
   research activities.  5. The maximum duration is 36 months, with budgets
   capped at 300,000 TL for theoretical research and 600,000 TL for
   experimental research (if lab/field costs exceed 30% of the budget).  6.
   Faculty can apply only once.   Note: Priority is given to infrastructure
   investments (equipment, consumables) over personnel salaries.
------------------------------------------------------------
